## Z. The detailed tier and DV4's step 1 as built (DV3b, DV4)

### Δ160 · The DV3b tools items as built: the solver swap and the brittleness flags, per-item construct forms, and open statements in the freeze pin — adopted (DV3b, 2026-10-06)
- **Status:** builds QPLAN §6 Q29, Q43 and Q44, which Δ159 assigned to the DV3b tools lane, and is item (d) of §6 Q46. It marks Δ159's first **Open** item. Q30 stays open. The integration lane's later changes to the empty-body gate's hole kinds are in Δ167.
- **Change:**
  - **The solver swap (Q29; `scripts/vcheck`, `scripts/dvl/dafny.py`).** `--solver S` runs a Dafny target in the learner loop under another bundled Z3 (with `--seed`, `--json` and `--module` probes). `--brittleness --solvers` and `--brittleness --solver S` sweep Dafny over the two Z3 builds bundled with Dafny 4.11.0, 4.12.1 (the pin) and 4.14.1, each pinned by path, version and sha256 in `scripts/dvl/pins.toml` `[dafny.solvers]` (the values of `VERSIONS.md` §1.1; a test checks that they agree) and checked once per process. The options are refused (exit 3) under `--gate`, with `--ref` outside `--brittleness`, and on Verus or Python targets, and are a usage error (exit 2) with `--test`, `--shipped`, `--pin-counts` or `--selftest`. A symbol whose resource spread exceeds a coefficient of variation of 0.20 (`spread_flag_cv`, per solver; two solvers' counts are never mixed) is flagged on one added line, `spread above 20%: …`, and never gates; the JSON gains `solvers`, `flagged`, `flag_cv` and a per-row `by_row`. The plain sweep's text output is unchanged except for that line and the order of equal spreads (now by symbol name, ascending), which moved the seed-rerun hashes of two cheat-corpus controls (re-pinned, Δ167). `vcheck --ref --brittleness [--solvers]` (author-only) lays a twin over its learner unit and prints locations, never symbol names (DV0-REPORT §8 items 18 and 37).
  - **Per-item construct forms (Q43; `scripts/dvl/forms.py`, `scripts/firsttry.py`).** A learner H, X or T block may carry `// form: <rule>[, <rule>]` lines, and its twin's block carries the same rule set; the vocabulary is `bv-exec`, `no-nl`, `nl-steps:<k>`, `trigger` and `stable`. A form line may sit anywhere in the block after the marker's `uses:`, `need:` and `pair:` lines, which must still follow the marker directly. An item's rules are the union of its current block's form lines and its shipped block's, so deleting a form line does not lift it. `bv-exec` and `no-nl` read the whole block and the same-file helpers, outside every item block, that it calls by name, transitively; `bv-exec` also rejects integer multiplication, division, remainder and power method calls, and `no-nl` also rejects `integer_ring` and a nonlinear attribute. `nl-steps` takes a count (a bare `nl-steps` is rejected). `nl-steps` and `trigger` read only the block's answer region (the block without the item's own declaration header and without the declarations it shipped with), so a trigger in a frozen statement or in given code is not the answer's. `stable` holds when, in every row of the sweep (Verus: seeds 1–3; Dafny: seeds 1–3 under both solvers), every declaration of the block and every declaration of the same file outside all item blocks verifies with no error in the block, and the block holds no resource attribute; it is judged per block, so another item's open hole in the same unit cannot fail it.
  - **Where the forms are checked.** `--check`: a code test-out's first try fails on any form, and an out-of-resource result inside the block now records a failed first try (outside the block, or a wall-clock kill, still records nothing). `--exit`: a reason per failing form; `stable` sweeps run only for blocks that otherwise pass. `--report`: a finished block whose forms fail is a `form` finding (the summary gains "forms not met N"); `stable` runs only under `--strict`, once per unit, crate or file. `--lint-hollow` checks the form lines and flags any `cfree:` or `triv:` line in a learner file.
  - **The construct-free gate.** A twin block may carry `// cfree: { … }` lines, each a construct-free answer the author measured. `firsttry --empty-body` (and `--eb-modes FORMS` alone) requires the twin's form lines to equal the learner's, the twin to meet its forms, and each construct-free answer to fail verification or a form; one that verifies and meets every form is reported CONSTRUCT NOT REQUIRED and fails the gate.
  - **Open statements in the freeze pin (Q44; the shared core of `scripts/vcheck`, `scripts/specfreeze`, `scripts/reference_lint.py`).** A `[[file]]` of a freeze pin may carry `open_specs = [...]` and a `[file.spec_clauses]` table (`expected/README.md`). `specfreeze --pin` takes the open specs from the twins (a declaration whose twin header differs from the shipped one by added `requires`/`ensures` clauses only) and prints them. `--check` requires the spec core (the signature and every other clause) to match exactly and the shipped `requires` and `ensures` to remain, in order; added clauses are accepted, and a missing `spec_clauses` entry counts as `freeze-changed`. Symbol names in the pin are file-level. `reference_lint.py` compares an open-spec symbol by its core: a five-line change in a file outside the lane's list, without which the twins of Q07.H7v and Q08.T1v and H3v report `statement-differs` (a test fails without it).
- **Why:** the tools lane's runs, one solver job at a time under the caps: the static wrapper suites 232 tests OK, 1 skipped (22 new, in `tests/wrappers/test_forms_static.py`; one assertion of `test_tools_static.py` follows the new summary line); `test_forms_tool.py` 7 OK in 125 s against the pinned Dafny and Verus (both sweeps, the solver hash check, `--ref --brittleness` on a twin, a test-out that fails and then meets the `trigger` form, the strict report's `stable` sweep, and the forms gate); every `*_tool.py` suite 138 OK in 740 s; the runner tests 15 OK. On Q01–Q06, `specfreeze --check`, `firsttry --lint-hollow` and `reference_lint.py` are unchanged, and no Q01–Q06 file has an open spec, so no earlier freeze pin changes. The integration lane's CI runs (Δ167) re-ran every suite.
- **Touches:** Δ10, Δ17, Δ44, Δ45, Δ47, Δ103, Δ137, Δ141, Δ159; QPLAN §6 Q29, Q30, Q43, Q44, Q46, §14.0; `VERSIONS.md` §1.1; `scripts/vcheck`, `scripts/dvl/dafny.py`, `scripts/dvl/forms.py`, `scripts/dvl/pins.toml`, `scripts/firsttry.py`, `scripts/specfreeze`, `scripts/reference_lint.py`, `expected/README.md`, `tests/wrappers/`.
- **Owner:** DV3b (the tools lane).
- **Open** (owner, severity):
  - **The tools owner (medium).** `open_specs` accepts added `requires` and `ensures` clauses only. A correct direct answer to one Dafny exit item of Q08 adds a clause of another kind to its shipped lemma and so reads as `freeze-changed` under rubric line A5; that item's twin proves it through a reference-only helper meanwhile (Δ165). `Solutions/plans/Q08-code-notes.md` §7 names the item and the clause, which the freeze should accept. **Built** (Δ168): a `decreases` added where none shipped is no change to the freeze.
  - **The tools owner (low).** Q30: `vcheck --test` prints no test's own output, so Q07's check unit is read from a `--json` run's raw directory (Δ164).
  - **The tools owner (low).** The forms are presence checks, a tripwire: a helper inside another item's block or in another file is not followed; `trigger` and `nl-steps` check that the construct is present, not that it carries the proof (the registry's `# nominal:` lines and the rubric say so); Dafny's `@`-style trigger attribute is not recognised.

### Δ161 · DV4 step 1a as built: `fvh.verdicts`, `fvh.corpus`, `fvh.tools` and the recorder — adopted (DV4, 2026-10-06)
- **Status:** builds Δ145 (DV4-PLAN §3.7 and §11 step 1a) and marks its first **Open** item. The interfaces are Δ145's, stable; the additions below are backward-compatible. The Q09 lane's recording and frozen tests are Δ166. The recorder's guard rows are step 1e's (Δ162).
- **Change:**
  - **The modules** (frozen core, rule 2f; standard library only; imported by submodule):
    - `fvh.verdicts`: `PASS`, `FAIL`, `UNKNOWN`, `GATE_WORDS`; `XCLASSES`, a read-only mapping of X01–X18 to spec §5.6's titles verbatim (a test reads the spec's table); `GRADER_ONLY` = X07, X08 and X14. Data only, no gate logic.
    - `fvh.corpus`: the frozen dataclasses `Delivery` (id, language, half, target, files: repo-relative POSIX paths, no label), `Expect`, `Label` and `Fixture`; `load_half(lang, half, *, root=None, covered=False)`, the fixtures that hold a program (constructs and controls) in `scripts/test_corpus.py`'s order, with the covered-elsewhere rows on request; `CorpusError` for an absent corpus or an unreadable manifest. A core test holds it to `test_corpus.py`'s loader on every fixture (ids, halves, classes, kinds, titles, targets, files, expectations).
    - `fvh.tools`: `INSTRUMENTS` (the six of `tests/corpus/README.md`); `ToolResult`, frozen and validated, whose `killed` is `None`, `memory` or `wall-clock`, and a killed result always carries the text form and never exit 0 (Δ57, Δ58); documents redacted as `scripts/pins.py` redacts, and `output_sha256` by the README's hash rule; the `Runner` interface with `HostRunner` (the course tools from the course root, one solver job at a time, a per-call scratch outside the tree, a process-group kill at its time-out, a memory preflight of the pinned cap plus 15 GB, and `ProvenanceError` on an agent target), `ContainerRunner` (isolated; every call raises `RunnerUnavailable` until step 2) and `ReplayRunner` (recordings indexed by instrument and target; `NoRecording` when none matches); `run`, `replay`, `provenance_of`, and `command` (README-form argvs, equal to `test_corpus.command` on every fixture).
  - **The provenance rule as built.** A target outside the course root, the root itself and every path under `research/` are `agent`; `tests/corpus/**` and `harness/tests/q/fixtures/**` are `course`; the given layers the registry declares (with their crates) and challenge statements are `course`; the registry's exercise, exit, agentic, harness and unit paths are `learner`; anything else is `agent`, and so is everything outside the two course trees when the registry cannot be read. `run` may raise a provenance and never lowers one, and refuses an agent target on a runner that is not isolated.
  - **The recorder** `python -m fvh.tools.record --half Q|R [--lang L] [--only ID…] [--instrument NAME…] [--out DIR] [--dry-run]`: self-gated as Δ103 asks (exit 3 outside the author's context); it writes `<dir>/<lang>/<ID>/<slug>.json` holding exactly `schema`, `instrument`, `target`, `argv`, `exit_code`, `killed` (always null), `provenance`, `output_sha256` and `document`, records only exits 0 and 1 (never a kill, a usage error or a refusal), and after a full recording of a fixture removes its stale recordings.
  - **Deviations from DV4-PLAN §3.7.** (1) The recorder records every selected instrument whose inputs exist for the delivery and never reads the label, where the plan said "every instrument the manifest lists": many manifests list only the instrument that catches their construct, so a manifest-driven recording would leave a gate's replays incomplete, and which recordings exist would encode the label. (2) `ContainerRunner` is a stub that raises until step 2, where the plan had it built up to its manifest and argv against a fake `scripts/mksandbox-run` at step 1a: the manifest needs step 1b's sandbox library and step 2a's image lock, so that part moves to step 2b. (3) The provenance details above, which the plan left open. (4) The additions: `run`'s keyword `provenance`, `load_half`'s `covered`, `ReplayRunner`'s default directory and `root`, `Runner.isolated`, `HostRunner`'s preflight switch and `argv`, the names `command`, `slug`, `provenance_of`, `KILLS`, `PROVENANCES`, `RUNNERS`, and the exceptions `RunnerUnavailable`, `PreflightError` and `NoRecording`.
  - **Next to Q09's holes.** No function of `fvh.tools` reads a result as flagged or clean (a test enforces this), and the helper that lists an instrument's input files is private, so that the frozen core does not answer Q09's gate-core holes (QPLAN §1.1 rule 3). Δ167's **Open** has one residual.
- **Why:** the step 1a lane's runs: `pytest tests/core` 242 passed (the 145 earlier core tests and 97 new: `test_verdicts` 6, `test_corpus` 22, `test_tools` 56, `test_record` 13), also with `CI=true` and in the sterile shape of step 1e; a live `HostRunner` run of five instruments on two corpus fixtures (Dafny only, 8 GB cap), each agreeing with its manifest, redacted, its scratch removed, and repeatable to the same document and hash; the recorder's dry run in the authoring worktree (140 results planned for the 43 Q-half fixtures, a superset of every instrument the manifests list) and one fixture recorded end to end, then removed. No container was started and no model called.
- **Touches:** Δ57, Δ58, Δ103, Δ108, Δ134, Δ136, Δ145; spec §5.6; DV4-PLAN §3.7, §11, §13; `tests/corpus/README.md`; `harness/fvh/verdicts.py`, `harness/fvh/corpus.py`, `harness/fvh/tools/`, `harness/tests/core/`.
- **Owner:** DV4 (the step 1a lane).
- **Open** (owner, severity):

### Δ162 · DV4 steps 1b–1e as built: the sandbox runner's checks, the benchmark fetcher, the replay's front end, the guard rows and the CI tripwires — adopted (DV4, 2026-10-06)
- **Status:** builds DV4-PLAN §11 steps 1b–1e and, of the decisions Δ149–Δ153, every part that needs neither Docker nor a model: Δ151's self-test, Δ152's manifest (all five sets `fetch`), Δ153's report-only access check and single-route check, Δ149's `.gitignore` check and `/contract` refusals, and Δ150's tracking rule in `.gitignore`. Steps 2a–2d (images, live runs, the route probe of Δ146, the sandbox doors), 3a–3b (call accounting, `contract_local.sh` end to end) and 4 (Δ147, Δ148) wait on host action H1 (Δ156). Q36 of QPLAN §6 (Δ159): the lane wrote `CLAUDE.md`'s **Not built yet** line, which names the live container runs of `scripts/mksandbox-run` and `scripts/contract_local.sh`.
- **Change:**
  - **`scripts/mksandbox-run` and `scripts/dvl/sandbox.py`.** Manifest schema 1 (TOML or JSON): a run id, the module, `kind` agent or verify, an image by digest from `sandbox/images.lock`, `network` none, ollama or anthropic (a verify run: none), limits within the pinned `[container]` bounds, files each with its role and sha256 (at most one snapshot, always with its revision), the returned paths, and for an agent run its CLI, model and turn cap; unknown keys are refused. Modes: `--dry-run` (prints the docker argv), `--selftest-only`, `--materialize` (a 0700 run directory under `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/dvl-runs/`), `--release`, `--records`, `--check-access` (a read-only `docker version`; on a refusal, a diagnosis read from `id` and `getent` and exit 3, never escalating, Δ153), and the self-gated `--author --attest` and `--author --selftest-only` (Δ103). A live run is refused after every check (exit 3) until step 2b. The self-test (Δ151): closure, canaries (`sandbox/canaries.sha256` is a documented empty list until the canaries are placed, DV4-PLAN §14 Q5), attested task cards (`sandbox/attested.toml`), public answers, and in the author's context the reference fingerprints. The return path parses the returned tar stream (links, devices, path escapes, repeats, oversize and non-UTF-8 names rejected and reported by size and sha256 only) into a git-style diff; a secret scan runs over it.
  - **The public-answer windows** are 32 tokens and 4 windows, where DV4-PLAN [A] said 24 and 3. Calibrated against the five installed sets (3,647 answers): at 24 tokens, shipped Q01–Q06 files share up to 10 windows with public answers through generic idioms and would be flagged; at 32 tokens, none. The task's own shipped form is excluded for a gitignored benchmark task (the installed, digest-pinned copy), without which derived tasks of one set are flagged against another set's answers.
  - **`run_capped`'s container modes** (`scripts/dvl/common.py`; `DVL_CAP=container-verify|container-agent`, host mode unchanged): refused unless the cgroup's `memory.max` is set and within the pinned bound (`pins.toml` `[container]`: margin 1, work 0.5, tmp 1.5 and home 0.5 GB, and 4 GB more in agent mode); a rise of the cgroup's `oom_kill` count during a job maps its exit to 137 (whatever the driver's own code: the conservative superset), the wall clock to 124; agent mode keeps one solver job across pools.
  - **`scripts/get_bench.sh`** (a front end of `scripts/dvl/bench.py`) and **`bench/manifest.toml`** (schema 1): the five sets of DV4-PLAN §7.3, each `fetch` (Δ152), pinned to a commit with its tree digest, its licence record, layout rows, kept licence and notice files, and subset rules; `--pin` is author-only; `--validate` runs `vcheck` on installed tasks; `vendor` is refused unless every licence is permissive and a leak scan is recorded. The five sets are installed in the authoring worktree (gitignored, not vendored), their upstream heads equal to the pinned commits, and a fresh install reproduces each digest. One set's copy leaves out a source that is a near-answer for the spine, and every Lean file; one installs answers only until DV5's stripping step; one installs only its licence and source record.
  - **`scripts/contract_local.sh`** (a front end of `scripts/dvl/contract_local.py`): `<ID> [--file P] [--model M] [--from REV] [--available | --dry-run]`. `--available` reports through the access check and the apparatus pins (exit 3 today: H1 is not done and the apparatus file does not exist); `--dry-run` parses the agent file's CONTRACT (mirroring the `/contract` skill's §1–§2), classifies its acceptance lines (vcheck-gate, zero-holes, specfreeze-against, vaudit, vaudit-compare, git-diff, audit), finds the starting state from the snapshot's commit and builds the run's manifest. It does not call `fvh contract-local` (fvh's protocol, budget, apparatus and contract modules come with later steps), and the replay's model list stays in the script until `sandbox/apparatus.toml` exists.
  - **The guard (step 1e;** `.claude/hooks/spoiler-guard.sh`, its fixtures regenerated from their templates): rule 1b's author rows add `mksandbox-run --author`/`--attest`, `get_bench`/`bench.py --pin` and the recorder in every launcher spelling; rule 2c (a contract active) refuses, line by line, `contract_local`, `get_bench`/`bench.py`, `fvh run`, `resume` and `contract-local`, and `mksandbox-run` other than `--check-access` and `--dry-run`; rule 1e adds the Claude Code credentials file and the sandbox's run state; rule 2f adds `bench/manifest.toml` and `bench/public/**`; rule 2 refuses an allowlist that names `research/runs/**` or the ledger anchors. `CLAUDE.md`'s access matrix, `CORE.md` and the `/contract` skill (whose §2 refuses those paths and the benchmark manifest and public sets as allowed files) follow.
  - **CI (step 1e;** `scripts/ci.sh`): `static` gains `scripts/check_single_route.py` (only allowlisted files name a container tool, an agent CLI or `sudo`; code files only), a check of the sandbox's refused source classes against the guard's and the checker's reference paths, and a check that `.gitignore` leaves the ledger anchors, the ledgers and `run.json` tracked (Δ149, Δ150). `harness` runs in a sterile environment (empty HOME, XDG and `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`; `FVH_NO_MODEL=1`, `DVL_NO_DOCKER=1`; the fake apparatus file when it exists; shims for `claude`, `opencode`, `ollama`, `docker` and `sudo` whose markers fail the job), under `harness/tests/conftest.py`'s socket tripwire (an unregistered connection or a non-local name lookup fails the test, even when the test swallows the error), and adds the sandbox and benchmark suites. `dvl.python`'s run environment passes `UV_CACHE_DIR` through, so the adapter suite runs offline under the sterile HOME.

Manifest details the plan left open: inline text only for the task, contract and project roles; `return` as a list of paths; `mem_gb` defaulting to, and never above, the pinned bound. `mksandbox-run` adds `--release`, `--records` and a hidden test-only `--root` (refused for live runs); the three front ends refuse abbreviated options. The benchmark manifest adds layout, keep and subset rows. The 2c refusal is line-level, reads included (an accepted cost, written into the hook header). No canary is planted yet.

### Δ163 · Layer L4 and the Q07–Q09 registry records as built — adopted (DV3b, 2026-10-06)
- **Status:** builds QPLAN §14.2–§14.4, §15.7, §16.7 and §17.11, with Δ157 and Δ158. L4 holds the re-supplied lower-tier proofs that Δ130 keeps. Q08's admissible entries were admitted by the integration lane (Δ165, Δ167).
- **Change:**
  - **L4, Dafny:** `dafny/given/Q/QG_L4_BigNat.dfy` (tier `std`): `QG_L4_BigNat` (importing L1–L3 opened and `Std.Arithmetic.Mul`), the abstract `QG_L4_Spec`, and `QG_L4_Limbs`, which refines it; each module carries `{:disableNonlinearArithmetic}` (QPLAN §14.0). Its declarations are QPLAN §14.2's, statements as listed there. A refiner imports L1–L4 by name, as `QG_L4_Limbs` does, so that their names cannot clash with the refined members. The code is the author notes' verified candidate except in two places: one lemma call in `AddLoop`'s loop body moved below an assertion of the body, because the file as recorded had a flagged resource spread under the other bundled solver and Q09's `stable` form sweeps files that reach L4 (after the move: stable under both solvers and seeds 1–3, nothing flagged); and one section comment no longer describes nonlinear arithmetic (QPLAN §14.1 rule 7; the module attribute states it). The author notes are marked where they differ (author-only).

It uses `vstd::arithmetic::mul` privately, so every Verus file that reaches it is tier `arithmetic` or wider (the check is transitive, Δ77). `use qg_bignat_l4::*;` brings L1–L3 too; `Ordering` is not re-exported.

Its regression case is the integration lane's (Δ167).

### Δ167 · DV3b and DV4 step-1 integration: the registry, the mechanical fixes, the empty-body gate's hole kinds, the re-pins, CI, the leak scan and the open items — adopted (DV3b, DV4, 2026-10-06)
- **Status:** the integration lane's record of DV3b (L4, the tools items, Q07–Q09's code) and DV4's step 1. Q07–Q09 stay `authoring`; their chapters, written pairs, rubrics, banks and leak patterns are still to come. It marks Δ144, Δ145, Δ154 and Δ159 in place.
- **Change:**
  - **The registry.** Q08's `[module.admissible]` entries are admitted (H5v 6 minutes, H6v 8 minutes, fallbacks kept: Δ165); no other field changed, since the spine lane's records match every marker as built. `check_course_map.py`: "course map OK: 49 modules (33 Q, 16 R; 0 shipped, 9 authoring, 40 planned); imports: 77 files; coverage: 81 rows (165 pending); ledger: 0 tag(s), all known", and every `--only` group passes (import tiers: 77 files in 6 tiers; isolation: 76 Q files and 1 R file; coverage: 9 modules past planned; vocabulary: 87 tags and 13 aliases). `gen_units.py --check`: 19 units match. `--items` parses Q07 to 37 items (7 with slots), Q08 to 33 (6) and Q09 to 35 (15). Every H, T and X item of QPLAN §15.2, §16.2 and §17.2 exists in its planned language, so Δ155's shares stand as planned.
  - **The empty-body gate's hole kinds** (`scripts/firsttry.py`, on the module lanes' proposals; static tests in `tests/wrappers/test_forms_static.py`, one gate run per affected item):
    - `static_holes` learns the Verus spec-definition hole `uninterp spec fn` (QPLAN §14.0; the tools count it, Δ8), so `--lint-hollow`, `scripts/ci.sh`'s per-item hole comparison, `--report` and `--empty-body` see Q07.H8v (a test in `test_tools_static.py`, failing on the old finder).
    - A **statement hole** (QPLAN §14.0: the labelled assume of a given client, in a block that opens with a bodyless trait method) and a **spec-definition hole** are reported as skipped by the shipped gate, with the reason, never silently: QPLAN §6 Q40 decides this for the statement holes (Q08.T1v, H3v); a spec-definition hole has no body to empty, and its definition is judged through its uses and the freeze pin (Q07.H8v).
    - An **open statement** (a declaration under its file's `open_specs`, Δ160) runs its shipped gate on a shadow of its crate whose file carries the twin's clauses over the shipped declaration, so the gate judges a body against the statement the hole poses rather than against none (Q07.H7v: every mode and seed then fails).
    - When every variant that verifies is a whole-goal NL or BV variant and each fails a static form of the item, the gate holds: the variant is a construct-free answer, judged as `cfree:` answers are (QPLAN §6 Q43; Q09.H1v and X1v).
  - **Mechanical fixes:**
    - `scripts/check_single_route.py` flagged a Q07 agent file's comment that named the local agent's CLI; the comment is reworded.
    - Four of Q06's chapter pins were stale because their input `scripts/dvl/pins.toml` changed (the tools and DV4 lanes' tables); re-captured with `pins.py --update --chapter Q06`; the fence bodies are unchanged, only the input hashes moved.
    - Two Verus controls of the cheat corpus (C-2, C-3) failed `test_corpus.py --ci` on their `seed-rerun` hash, because the brittleness sweep's text gained the flag line and a new order of equal spreads (Δ160); re-pinned with `test_corpus.py --update` (author-only), each verdict unchanged.
    - `scripts/test_course_map.py`'s fixtures (no tool changed for them): `_inline` renders the nested inline tables of `[module.admissible]`; a real module planned back in an armed case also loses the given layer it declares (L4, with Q07 planned); the cases that used Q07 or Q08 as planned stand-ins use Q10 and Q11; the package count after an overlay leaves out a package nested below a materialized directory, which the checker's glob does not descend there; and a new case covers the spine lane's sort fix (Δ163), failing with the old checker's `TypeError`. 363 of 363 pass.
    - `tests/wrappers/test_tools_static.py`: the refusal case for a planned module names Q10.

## AA. The detailed tier's chapters and kits (DV3b kits phase)

### Δ168 · Added measures in the freeze, and the review of the empty-body gate's hole kinds — adopted (DV3b kits phase, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** builds Δ160's first **Open** item (the measure clause the Q08 lane asked the freeze to accept), Δ167's tools-owner review of the empty-body gate's hole kinds, and Δ166's item on `dvl.python`'s overlay; each is marked in place. Q30 and the forms' presence-check residuals stay open (Δ160). Like Δ160–Δ167, this entry names no answer, no exit statement and no tool behaviour that a prediction asks about.
- **Change:**
  - **Added measures** (`scripts/vcheck`'s shared open-spec core, `scripts/specfreeze`, `scripts/reference_lint.py`). When a statement differs from its pin or its twin, it is compared again with the candidate's header `decreases` clauses blanked: a `decreases` added to a declaration that shipped without one is no change. A shipped measure stays frozen (a pin that holds one no longer matches once the candidate's is blanked), and two clause forms are never blanked: Dafny's `decreases *` and a Verus clause with `when` or `via`. The rule is general, not tied to `open_specs`, because the exit item in question proves its lemma without an `open_specs` entry: it holds for `specfreeze --check`, `--against` (`dvl freeze_check`, both languages), `reference_lint.py` and the open-spec detection behind `--pin`. No freeze pin changed (Q07–Q09 re-render identically; Q01–Q06 differ only in the header comment that Δ160's renderer already wrote). `expected/README.md`'s `.freeze` row says so (Δ172), and Q08's rubric line A5 accepts it.
  - **The review of the empty-body gate's hole kinds** (`scripts/firsttry.py`; Δ167 built them on the module lanes' proposals). Confirmed: `static_holes`' `uninterp spec fn`; the statement and spec-definition skips; an open statement gated on the twin's clauses (a shadow that does not resolve gives verdict `error`, never a pass); the forms judgement of whole-goal NL/BV variants. Two corrections: a statement hole is recognised whatever the mode of the bodyless trait method that opens its block (it needed a `spec fn`, so an `exec` opener would have been refused loudly instead of skipped), and a block is skipped only when every hole form in it is of a skipped kind (a block that also held another hole would have been skipped silently; now the refusal stays loud). One bug fixed: for an open statement, the forms judgement of whole-goal variants now reads the shadow text that carries the twin's clauses, the text the gate ran. The decisions on Q01–Q09 are unchanged (Q07.H8v skipped as a spec-definition hole, Q08.T1v and H3v as statement holes, nothing else).
  - **`dvl.python`'s overlay** copies `tests/corpus/` (a copy, not a symlink) when it exists, so the Q09 acceptance tests' check of their table against the corpus runs in `vcheck --ref` and the TRIV mode too (it was skipped there).
  - **Tests** (`tests/wrappers/test_forms_static.py`): `AddedMeasures` (×4: an added Dafny lemma or method measure, a Verus proof fn and spec fn measure accepted; a changed or removed shipped measure, `decreases *`, a Verus `when`, and an added measure beside a changed `ensures` refused), two `EmptyBodyHoleKinds` cases, `PythonOverlay`.
- **Why:** the tools lane's runs: `tests/wrappers` static 243 OK (1 skipped), tool 138 OK in 781 s (pinned Dafny and Verus, one job at a time), common 15 OK; `tests/ci` 45 OK; `specfreeze --check` 0 findings in Q01–Q09; `reference_lint.py` 0 findings over 82 twins; `--lint-hollow --all` OK; on a scratch copy, the real Q08 exit item with an added measure gives 0 findings and with `decreases *` gives `freeze-changed`; `eb_skip_reason` before and after over every hole item of Q01–Q09, the same decisions; both Python twins' reference gates and TRIV modes (Q09's corpus-parity test now runs and passes).
- **Touches:** Δ160, Δ165, Δ166, Δ167; QPLAN §6 Q40, Q43, Q44; `scripts/vcheck`, `scripts/specfreeze`, `scripts/reference_lint.py`, `scripts/firsttry.py`, `scripts/dvl/dafny.py`, `scripts/dvl/verus.py`, `scripts/dvl/verus_spec.py`, `scripts/dvl/python.py`, `tests/wrappers/test_forms_static.py`, `expected/README.md`, `paper/rubrics/Q08_rubric.md`.
- **Owner:** the DV3b kits phase (the tools lane).
- **Open** (owner, severity):
  - **Q08's owner (low, optional).** The Q08 exit twin that proves its lemma through a reference-only helper (Δ165) may now use the direct answer; `reference_lint.py` and `--pin` accept that twin, and no pin would change.
  - **The tools owner (low).** A Dafny open-statement hole item, if one is ever added, is gated on its shipped clause-less form (no twin-clause shadow): it would fail loudly as FALLS TO AUTOMATION, never pass silently. No current module has one.
  - **Process (low).** Early in its run the tools lane ran `git stash` / `git stash pop` once in the shared authoring worktree; the pop was clean (no conflict, empty stash list, untracked files untouched). The integration lane's runs on the final tree (Δ172) cover every lane's tracked edits.

### Δ169 · The frozen core and the gate core: `fvh.tools.command` stays public, and a gate may compose it — adopted (DV3b kits phase, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** decides Δ167's **Open** item on the leak scan's one residual between the frozen core and a gate-core hole, and closes the residual of Δ166's **Open** item on `Delivery.id`; both are marked in place, as is the author notes' §9 item. Δ145's and Δ161's stable interfaces are unchanged.
- **Change:**
  - **The decision.** `fvh.tools.command` keeps its public status, signature and behaviour. The docstrings of `harness/fvh/tools/__init__.py` and `runners.py` say that `command`'s paths besides the target are the companion files of `tests/corpus/README.md`'s public layout, that a gate may compose `command`, and that whether a delivery brings those files, and what a result means, is the gate's own work. `_input_paths` stays private. The alternative, making `command` private, would hide nothing (the layout is public in `tests/corpus/README.md` and in H4py's own docstring, and the frozen core is learner-readable, so a private name stays callable), would change Δ145's stable interface and the core's tests, and would save the learner only the path joins.
  - **H4py re-posed in its docstring only**, identically in the learner file and the twin: the layout is public, `fvh.tools.command(instrument, target)` encodes it and may be composed instead of writing the paths out, and the rest is the gate's work (whether the delivery brings an instrument's companions, a companion directory counting when a delivery file lies under it, and a verdict from the results alone). The reference body and `expected/Q09.freeze` are unchanged (the freeze hashes no docstring).
  - **One acceptance test added**, `test_h4_decides_from_the_layout_not_the_names` (70 in all, frozen): every delivery is moved to a neutral path under a neutral id, keeping its layout, and must get the same verdict and the same instruments. A gate that branches on a control's path or id prefix passed every earlier test and fails this one.
  - **The rubric and the chapter** (Δ171, Δ170): Q09's rubric line C4 accepts either route for the companions (the layout written out, or `command` composed); the chapter says the layout is public and that composing `command` is allowed, and states no recipe beyond the docstring.
  - **Records.** `Solutions/plans/Q09-code-notes.md` §1, §4 and §11 (69 → 70 tests; 66 failing as shipped).
- **Why:** the Q09 follow-up lane's runs: `vcheck --ref` on the twin, 69 passed and 1 skipped at the time (the corpus check, before Δ168's overlay copy), 0 holes; the learner file as shipped, 4 passed, 66 failed, 4 holes; `pytest tests/core` 246 passed (the word ban on the tools files included); TRIV 4/4; on all 85 deliveries of both halves and languages, instruments chosen by composing `command` (all non-target paths present, a directory counting through a file under it) differ from the reference's choice on none; scratch mutants: a gate that branches on the control prefix fails only the new test, a composing gate that requires every path passes, one that accepts any path fails 5 tests; `pins.py --ci` and `--ref`, `reference_lint.py`, `specfreeze --check`, `--report` and `vcheck --shipped Q09 py` on Q09, each clean; `ci.sh --author --job harness` PASS.
- **Touches:** Δ145, Δ161, Δ166, Δ167; QPLAN §17.7; `harness/fvh/tools/__init__.py`, `harness/fvh/tools/runners.py`, `harness/q/fvh_q/gate/core.py`, `Solutions/harness/q/fvh_q/gate/core.py`, `harness/tests/q/gate/test_core.py`, `Solutions/plans/Q09-code-notes.md`, `Solutions/plans/Q07-Q09-author-notes.md` §9.
- **Owner:** the DV3b kits phase (the Q09 follow-up lane); DV4 for the core's docstrings.
- **Open:** none.

## AB. The practice tiers and the R track: the orchestrator's decisions on the plans (DV5, DVR0)

### Δ173 · Admission takes the four results; one line-count rule; a fallback carries minutes only on its own four results — adopted (orchestrator, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** the orchestrator's adoption of the plan review's rules as QPLAN §19.0 and PLAN §0 state them, for every item of Q10–Q18 and of the R track. It is the line-count and fallback part of QPLAN §30 Q47. It applies spec §2.8's admissibility gate and design §7.5's "gated items carry no minutes until DV5 admits them" and extends Δ53's reading of a reference's length to every item. It changes no shipped module (Q01–Q09 keep the rules they were built under).
- **Change:**
  - **The four results.** An item carries minutes only when its module lane has recorded, on the built files, the results its plan lists: QPLAN §19.0 for Q10–Q18 (the reference complete and inside its tier's line and invented-lemma bands, counted after the given layers re-supply their targets; the empty-body gate failing, Dafny under maximal automation and the shipped options at seeds 1–3, Verus in every light mode, an exec hole's trivial body too; the twin stable on seeds 1–3, and for Dafny under both bundled solvers; only pinned APIs) and PLAN §0 for R (the reference verifying on 3 seeds at the pins; the empty body failing in the shipped configuration; failing under maximal automation too, or the shipped lever named; a trivial body failing where the item is exec code). Each `// cfree:` line the author knows of must fail as well (QPLAN §19.0). Until then the item's `[module.admissible]` entry (Q) or minutes (R) are 0, with a named fallback, and its itemized line says *gated*. A plan's probes are evidence for the plan, never for the built files: the module lane runs the gate again before it admits an item.
  - **One line-count rule.** A reference's size is its proof text only: the non-blank, non-comment lines of proof blocks, assertions, loop invariants and `decreases` clauses, ghost and tracked declarations and updates, lemma calls and the bodies of proof functions and lemmas, plus the statements of every lemma or exec helper the learner must invent. Exec code is not counted. This is Δ53's count, now for every item of both tracks; the author notes give each probed item's count under it.
  - **The fallback rule.** A fallback takes an item's minutes only after it has its own four results, recorded in the author notes (a paper fallback: its rubric rows and model answer there). A fallback without them carries 0, and the module lane records the line's shortfall in a DELTAS note when it arms the module. On the R track a fallback never removes the module's only learner-generalized statement or the only item of a WRITE slug (PLAN §0).
  - **Declared band exceptions and a tripwire form** (added in place by Δ218: the user's decisions D2, D6, D7, D9 and D11, 2026-10-12). An admitted item outside its line band carries a declared exception, `exception = "<kind>: <why>"` in `[module.admissible]`, which `scripts/check_course_map.py` checks: `short-format band` (its minutes line is short by design: Q15.H1d/H1v, Q18.H1d/H1v) or `statement-item` (the learner writes its statements and a contractor their proof: the item sits in an agentic file, or its prose block names the contractor, an A item of the module in an agentic file, in a `// contractor:` line, Δ223: Q17.H3d, Q18.H2d/H2v). A statement item with no shipped hole takes `firsttry --empty-body`'s statement route in place of the empty-body result, and an admitted item with neither a hole nor that exception fails the gate (Δ223). Q15.H1d keeps its 8 minutes although its `trigger` form is a presence check (a tripwire): rubric C1's `/review-code` reader closes the padding (D11). D11 names that item only; no general rule for other forms is stated here.
- **Why:** the plan review of the DV5 and R plans (2026-10-07): one proposed admission had no gate run, line counts changed convention from item to item, and fallbacks carried minutes without a reference (QPLAN §19.0's revision note; `Solutions/plans/Q10-Q18-author-notes.md` §13; `Solutions/plans/R-author-notes.md` §10). The orchestrator's brief for this round adopts the rules.
- **Touches:** Δ53, Δ58, Δ133; spec §2.5, §2.8; design §7.5; QPLAN §19.0, §29, §30 Q47 and Q51; PLAN §0, §3 (R11's arena line), §6.
- **Owner:** the DV5 module lanes (Q10–Q18) and DVR1–DVR3 (R), each at its module's arming.
- **Open** (owner, severity):
  - **The module lanes (high: at each arming).** The four results for every item and fallback on the built files, and the shortfall notes. Q10–Q12: recorded (Δ186–Δ188).

## AC. The practice tiers' first sweep and the R spikes as built (DV5a, DVR0)

### Δ182 · The practice tiers' tool items as built: frozen imports, placeholder open bodies, the lab lever check, impl-method and tracked-let empty bodies, out-of-resource runs, and the learner loop's measures — adopted (DV5a, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** builds Δ181's first **Open** item (QPLAN §30 Q52, Q62, Q63, Q65, Q66), PLAN §8 R15(a) and the Q52 half of R11, and the two JSON fields R05.P3dv reads; Δ181's item is marked in place. It **amends Δ47's E0 family**: E0, E0H, E1, E2, EC and EM keep `let tracked` statements (Q65(b)). Like Δ160–Δ181, this entry names no answer, no exit statement and no tool behaviour that a prediction asks about.
- **Change:**
  - **Frozen imports (Q62).** A Dafny module that carries the comment line `// specfreeze: frozen-imports` keeps its import list, and every module nested in it keeps its own. `specfreeze --pin` writes a `[file.frozen_imports]` table (each module's imports as sha256s; their order is free); `--check` reports `freeze-imports-changed — module M` for an added, removed or changed import and `freeze-missing — module M` for a marked module that is gone; `--against` and `--lab` apply the frozen file's markers the same way; `reference_lint.py` reports `imports-differ`. Helpers in `scripts/dvl/dafny.py`: `FROZEN_IMPORTS_MARKER`, `module_imports`, `subtree_imports`, `frozen_import_modules`, `Source.import_decls`.
  - **Placeholder open bodies (Q52).** `--pin` lists under `open_bodies`, beside the bodyless ones, the Dafny functions and predicates shipped with a placeholder body that the twin replaces: their header is compared exactly and their body is ignored, in the freeze and in `reference_lint.py` (vcheck core `open_body_symbols`). A bodyless predicate without an `ensures` stays a `bodyless-silent` lint finding, an uncounted hole, as before: an item of that shape ships as a placeholder-bodied predicate plus a counted hole (R04.H4d, PLAN §8 R11).
  - **The lab gate's lever check (Q63).** New module `scripts/dvl/levers.py` (`lever_findings(task, delivery)`, `levers`, `LeverError`) compares a task form with its delivery, two files of one language or two directories, and reports `lab-lever-added`, `lab-lever-removed`, `lab-file-added` and `lab-project-changed`. A removed shipped lever is refused as well as an added one (dropping an automation-off attribute switches automation back on); removing a trust escape (`external_body`, `{:axiom}`, `{:extern}`, `{:verify}`, `{:only}`) is no finding, because an exec hole's escape must go. CLIs: `scripts/specfreeze --lab TASK DELIVERY [--json]` (the statement freeze plus the levers) and `scripts/vcheck --gate --lab-task TASK DELIVERY [--json]`, which adds the findings, turns `verified` into `failed` and keeps the verifier's exit code; a pair the check cannot compare gives `lab-levers-unchecked`.
  - **Empty bodies for impl methods and tracked code (Q65).** `dvl.verus.empty_body` takes `Type::method` and `<Type as Trait>::method` and refuses a bare name that several fns share, with a hint; its `--verify-function` argument is `Type::method` when a name is shared (unique names keep `*::method`, so Q05.H2v and Q08.X2v are unchanged; the patterns were chosen from runs at the pin). `firsttry --empty-body` gates a trait-impl item once per hole method, and a twin may name each method's trivial body as `// triv: <method>: { … }`. A proof fn that returns a value gets `{ arbitrary() }` as its empty body; a unit or tracked return keeps `{}`. The E0 family keeps `let tracked` statements and still strips ghost lets, asserts, proof blocks and invariants; no Q01–Q09 Verus file has a `let tracked`, so no earlier gate result changes.
  - **Out of resources (Q66, decided).** A Dafny empty-body run that ends `inconclusive`, the maximal-automation run included, counts as failing at the pin and is never lever-needed; the `empty-body-inconclusive` finding and `firsttry --empty-body` say so ("counted as failing (QPLAN §30 Q66)").
  - **Measures (R05.P3dv).** The learner loop's and the fast channel's `vcheck --json --measures` add `resource_count` (Dafny's summed ResourceCount, Verus's summed per-function rlimit count; null when the run left none) and `wall_s`. They never enter the gate's record, whose document `fvh`'s HostRunner hashes (Δ161). The lane built them into every learner-loop document; the integration lane made them opt-in behind `--measures` (Δ189), because six chapter pins of Q06, Q07 and Q09 quote the plain document.
- **Why:** this round's tools lane, on the authoring worktree: `tests/wrappers` static 272 OK (29 new in `test_practice_tools_static.py`), the tool suites 146 OK in 751 s against the pinned Dafny and Verus (8 new in `test_practice_tools_tool.py`), `tests/ci` 81 OK; `specfreeze --check` 0 findings for each of Q01–Q09 with `expected/Q0*.freeze` byte-identical; `reference_lint.py` 0 findings; `--lint-hollow` and the course map OK.
- **Touches:** Δ47, Δ161, Δ181; QPLAN §30 Q52, Q62, Q63, Q65, Q66; PLAN §8 R11, R15; `scripts/dvl/levers.py`, `dafny.py`, `verus.py`, `verus_spec.py`; `scripts/specfreeze`, `scripts/vcheck`, `scripts/firsttry.py`, `scripts/reference_lint.py`; `tests/wrappers/test_practice_tools_{static,tool}.py` and their fixtures; `expected/README.md` (Δ189).
- **Owner:** this round's tools lane.
- **Open** (owner, severity):
  - **DV4 (high: before Lab 1's and Lab α's runs).** `fvh`'s lab gate calls the lever check (`dvl.levers.lever_findings`, `specfreeze --lab --json` or `vcheck --gate --lab-task`); `fvh.tools.INSTRUMENTS` (frozen) has no lab instrument yet.
  - **The Q14 and Q17 lanes (medium: at their arming).** A trait-impl item's twin names each exec method's trivial body; whether the tracked E0 variants compile is checked on the built files.
  - **The R lane (medium: at R04's arming).** R04.H4d ships as a placeholder-bodied predicate with a counted hole.

### Δ183 · `avoid.sh`, the disjointness gate, as built; layer A1b in the generator and the checker — adopted (DV5a, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** builds Δ174's first **Open** item (R06's layer, the layer name in the checker and the generator, the `RG_A1b_` prefix) and Δ177's first (the waiver file), and applies Δ180 (curated names). Both are marked in place. It **supersedes PLAN §5.5's waiver file path and column order** (§5.5 left the path to this build).
- **Change:**
  - **The gate** `scripts/avoid.sh [--track Q|R|both] [--json] [--list-sources] [--extra-r PATH]…` (implementation `scripts/avoid/avoid.py`). Exit 0 clean; 1 an unwaived hit or a stale name waiver; 2 malformed input (a waiver, curated or owners line; an unreadable source; the item blocks of a non-planned module that do not parse; a tag or genre home missing in an armed module); 3 usage. A hit line names the key, the file and line, the source descriptor (`hole:`, `bank:`, `paper:`, `chapter:`, `curated:`, `artifact:`, `owner:`, `corpus:`, `self:`) and the hit kind (`name`, `curated`, `artifact`, `owner`, `half`, `self`).
  - **What it compares.** Each track's declarations against the other track's sources: the hole-bodied and posed declarations of its items (also the `need:` lines and backticked signatures of items written from nothing, and every declaration of an agent block), its banks, papers and chapters; the curated Lean names (`scripts/avoid.curated`: `name`, `type` or `never`, for Q, R or both); the owned-artifact word lists of design §9.2 item 3 inside the other track's keys; the prediction tags of §9.2 item 1, listed with their homes (the only name-level tripwire is `midpoint`; spec §2.7 claims no more); literal references to the other corpus half. A self-check compares a bank's parallel problems with the track's own hole-bodied or posed declarations. Keys are the last name component with the track, layer (`RG_A1b_` included) and lemma affixes stripped, camel and snake case split, lower case. `scripts/avoid/owners.toml` holds the owner lists and a `[conventions]` list of keys never compared as equal (`wf`, `view`, `new`, `len`, the Dafny failure-protocol names, the option and result constructors and the like, each documented there).
  - **Waivers** `scripts/avoid.waivers.tsv`, columns `id state key source direction reason cost`; a genre row has key `-` and its homes in `source`, and its item homes in armed modules must exist; `N<n>` ids mark name-only waivers; `state` is `adopted` or `provisional` (W19, Δ177). A name waiver is stale only when its target track has declarations in the run, and idle otherwise. The file holds W1–W19, X1 and N1. Both input files live under `scripts/`, guard class 2g, so no learner or contractor session can add a waiver.
  - **CI:** the static job runs `avoid.sh` (`scripts/ci.sh`). The R spikes (`--extra-r Solutions/spikes/regex`, counted as R declarations only, not as R sources) are scanned by hand, never by CI.
  - **Layer A1b:** `scripts/gen_units.py` (`R_LAYERS` with A1b, an index-based `layer_rank`, the crate `rg_re_a1b`, the Dafny prefix `RG_A1b_`), `scripts/check_course_map.py` (its given-file, import-prefix and crate patterns), `MODULES.toml` (R06's `layer = "A1b"` and the layer comment), four new cases in `scripts/test_course_map.py`. The wrappers' given-layer patterns in `scripts/dvl/dafny.py` and `verus.py` were made to accept A1b by the integration lane (Δ189).
- **Why:** the avoid lane: `avoid.sh` on the tree `OK` (Q 1375 declarations and 439 sources at the time, R none, 34 curated names, 0 hits); `tests/ci/test_avoid.py` 36 OK; the course map OK with R06 at A1b; `gen_units.py --check`; `test_course_map.py` 367 passed; `ci.sh --job static` PASS; `test_ci_static.py` 29 OK. The spikes lane renamed every name the spike scan hit; the final `--extra-r` run is OK with 0 hits and 1 waived (N1).
- **Touches:** Δ174, Δ177, Δ180; spec §2.7; design §9.2, §9.3; PLAN §5.5; `scripts/avoid.sh`, `scripts/avoid/`, `scripts/avoid.curated`, `scripts/avoid.waivers.tsv`, `scripts/ci.sh`, `scripts/gen_units.py`, `scripts/check_course_map.py`, `scripts/test_course_map.py`, `tests/ci/test_avoid.py`, `MODULES.toml`.
- **Owner:** this round's avoid lane.
- **Open** (owner, severity):

  - **The Q09 owner or DV4 (medium: before Q09 ships).** The Q09 gate CLI's `--half` accepts the R half's corpus; `avoid.sh` catches only literal references. Restrict it to Q.

### Δ191 · The `no-call:` and `formula:` form rules; Q10.H1d and Q11.H7d/H8d admitted — adopted (DV5a kits, 2026-10-07)
- **Status:** the orchestrator's decision (a) on Δ187's VC items, and Δ186's tools item.
- **Change:**
  - **`no-call:<f>`** (Dafny): the block's answer region calls `f` only inside a specification statement or clause (`assert`, `assume`, `calc`, `ghost`, `reveal`; `invariant`, `decreases`, `requires`, `ensures`, `reads`, `modifies`). **`formula:<p>`** (Dafny): the body of the declaration `p`, anywhere in the file, has no `var`, no `match` and no call. Both in `scripts/dvl/forms.py`, with four new static tests (`FormArgumentRules`).
  - **Targeted answers.** A twin line `// cfree[<p>]: { … }` (anywhere in the file) is a construct-free answer of the item whose block carries `formula:<p>`; firsttry's forms gate puts its body in `p`'s place (`body_variant(…, target=)`).
  - **Items.** Q10.H1d carries `// form: no-call:ToNat`; Q11.H7d and H8d carry `formula:Pre1` and `formula:Pre2`, and the Q11 twin's construct-free answers (the program called, the program restated with `var` bindings) are targeted lines. `firsttry --empty-body`: Q10 14/14, Q11 12/12, each recorded shortcut killed by its form. `MODULES.toml`: H1d 10, H7d 10, H8d 10 admitted; Q10's and Q11's hole lines are whole again.
  - **Chapters and rubrics** say which tool checks each rule (Q10 §1 and §6, Q11 §3 and §4; Q10 C1/A5, Q11 C4/A5).
- **Why:** each item's construct (a case analysis; a hand-computed formula) was otherwise checked by a reader only, and Δ173 gives no minutes to an item whose known shortcut passes.
- **Touches:** Δ173, Δ186, Δ187; QPLAN §30 Q62; the files above; `expected/Q10.chapter.toml`, `expected/Q11.chapter.toml` (the units pins re-captured: lines moved).
- **Owner:** the orchestrator; the tools owner reviews.
- **Open:** a loop-required form (Δ186) is still not built; nothing needs it now.

### Δ206 · The user's decisions D1–D4 (2026-10-12), replacing Δ204 items 1, 2 and 5 — adopted (the user, 2026-10-12)
- **Status:** the user's decisions on the orchestrator's open items of Δ204 and Δ205. **Δ204 items 1, 2 and 5 are replaced** by D1, D2 and D3; Δ196's, Δ199's and Δ205's open item on Q13.H5d's and Q14.H3v's minutes is closed by D4 (completed by Δ214). Applied by DV5c's decisions-and-tools lane (Δ207) and DV4's remainder lane (Δ208). Like Δ195–Δ205, it names no answer.
- **Change:**
  - **D1, the layout rule.** A proof block that holds one statement (Verus `proof { … }`; an assertion's `by { … }` in either language) counts as one proof line however it is laid out, for every item of both tracks (Δ173's line-count rule gains it). The counter is now a course tool, `scripts/pcount.py` (`--no-layout-rule` reproduces the author notes' C.8 counter on all 1,112 twin declarations). Every admitted item of Q10–Q15 was re-counted and none leaves its band: Q13.H1v 43 (it stays admitted at 35, now inside the 15–45 band on the rule), Q13.H3v 24, Q13.H5d 12, Q14.H2v 23, Q14.H3v 29, Q14.H5v 19, Q15.H1v 4, and four intro items one lower (Q10.H7v, Q11.H4v, Q12.H4v, Q12.H7v).
  - **D2, a declared band exception.** Q15.H1d and H1v are admitted at the S11 line's 15 minutes under a short-format band exception. `[module.admissible]` entries gain an optional key `exception = "short-format band: <why>"`, which `scripts/check_course_map.py` accepts only on an admitted item, only of a known kind and only with a reason (five `test_course_map.py` cases). **As built, the 15 minutes are the pair's**, split H1d 8 and H1v 7 as QPLAN §25.7 splits the line; reading D2 as 15 minutes for each item awaits the user's confirmation (Δ216 item 1).
  - **D3, policy mode keeps opencode** for both modes and both families' Qwen arms: DV4-PLAN §14 Q3's direct Ollama transport is not built. The title-call fix and the pinned options of Δ202 stay; Δ208 records their live check and the measured overhead.
  - **D4, the loop-direction form.** `dvl.forms` gains `loop-dir:[<x>:]up|down` (Dafny and Verus, static): a `while` loop's counters are the guard's identifiers its body assigns, each assignment a step up, a step down or another; a `for` loop is read by its range. `loop-dir:up` needs a loop and every loop of the answer region counting up; `loop-dir:<x>:up` every loop on `x` stepping `x` up only. Q13.H5d carries `form: loop-dir:up` and Q14.H3v `form: loop-dir:i:down`; each twin's recorded construct-free loop fails its form (`firsttry --empty-body` Q13 7/7, Q14 7/7). Δ214 extends the rule to the loop's indices and adds `no-call:ToNat` to Q13.H5d after a review found two variants that passed. **Admitted:** Q13.H5d 20 and Q14.H3v 13; Q13's H lines carry 140 of 140 and Q14's 150 of 150.
- **Why:** the user's decisions of 2026-10-12, on the lanes' recorded results (`Solutions/plans/Q13-code-notes.md` §11–§12, Q14 §13, Q15 §17; archive `dv5c/tools/`).

  - **`fvh report --reverify RUN|all`**: the run's inputs with `returned/` laid over them, re-gated in a fresh verification container at the run's `course_commit`. **Agent-mode `rederive`** without a container. `fvh.runlog.RUN_FILES` adds `calls.jsonl`, `gate.log.gz`, `gate-counts.json`, `sandbox.json`.

### Δ215 · Lab 3's harness pieces: the statements pin and `fvh unseal`, crate tasks, the gated arm, the exact-value secret scan; canaries planted; Q13's and Q17's prose references sealed; calibration over Q01–Q18; Lab 3's tasks validated — adopted (DV5c, 2026-10-12)
- **Status:** built and tested by the gaps lane of DV5c's Release B completeness review. Like Δ195–Δ205, it names no answer; the detail is in `Solutions/plans/DV5c-gaps-notes.md` (archive `dv5c/gaps/`).
- **Change:**
  - **QPLAN §30 Q64 built.** `expected/<ID>.statements` (frozen) pins each statement file's shipped form by sha256 (`expected/Q18.statements`). `fvh.statements.check` passes only for committed files whose content differs from the shipped form; `python -m fvh unseal <ID>` then checks, decrypts with the pinned age and the key the truth file names, verifies and writes a sealed stratum's cards, never over a differing file, with an `UNSEALED.toml` record. A lab plan whose task lies in a sealed stratum must name `statements`; the batch note and every run.json carry their committed hashes, and `fvh resume` refuses when they changed. (Rule 1c already refuses `unseal` in a study session.)
  - **Crate tasks.** A plan task may be a crate card's `tasks` directory: `src/**/*.rs` editable, its other files read-only companions staged at every verification (`Executor(support_files=)`); the lever check compares the two directories; `fvh report` re-derives and re-verifies crate runs.
  - **The gated arm** (QPLAN §28.5's arm G): `acceptance = "gate"` with the plan's `gate` and pre-registered `gate_sha256`: every Verify runs gate v2's `gate_crate` on the work copy (each instrument in a fresh verification container, provenance agent), a Stop is refused until the gate passes, and the run ends `gate-pass`; arm P (`acceptance = "verify"`, the default) is unchanged; the outcome is the plain lab gate's verdict in both arms. The semantics await the user's confirmation (Δ216 item 11).
  - **The exact-value secret scan** (DV4-PLAN §5.7): the Claude transport keeps its snapshots' access tokens in memory and the run-directory scan withholds any file that holds one (`exact-token`).
  - **Canaries** (DV4-PLAN §14 Q5): `DVLCANARY` files in the reference trees, the hidden truth, the author cache and each public set's `answers/` (written by `get_bench.sh` outside the set digest); `sandbox/canaries.sha256` lists 11 trees; `sandbox_doors.py canary` checks each.

### Δ219 · Agent-mode batches as built (D8): `fvh.batch`'s agent construct, `fvh.backends.agent`, arm G's stop in agent mode (D17), `dvl.sandbox`'s agent manifests and session keywords; the sandbox suite's job-directory leak — adopted (DV5d, 2026-10-12)
- **Status:** the user's decision D8 (Lab 3's Qwen arm; R16 later). Closes Δ216 item 12's Qwen half, Δ217's DV4 item "agent-mode batch composition" and Δ208's agent-mode item. Under D10 a Claude agent-mode arm stays refused until step 4c. Δ223 fixed the live flag, a killed session's spend and the refused stop's brief after a review. Like Δ195–Δ217, it names no answer. Detail: `Solutions/plans/DV4-author-notes.md` §12.
- **Change:**
  - **`fvh.batch`, construct `"agent"`** (Qwen family only; no policy; turns = rounds = the gate's call cap, at most 200; caps `wall_s` (at least 120 s, at most 14,400) and `gpu_s` only). `open()` preflights every agent arm × task: the session manifest is built and self-tested, and an unattested card refuses the batch. Each attempt stages the task by `src` (a file, or a crate's `src/**/*.rs` editable and its other files read-only). Each session gets the attempt's derived seeds. The returned work copy is judged in a fresh verification container: the lab gate with its lever check for a lab plan. RunMeta mode is `"agent"`, backend `qwen`; records go to `seg-<k>/` per session.
  - **Arm G in agent mode (D17):** the agent's stop is the arm's `Verify`. At each stop the plan's gate (`gate_crate`, each instrument in a fresh verification container, provenance agent) runs on the work copy. A pass ends the run `gate-pass`. A failure refuses the stop: a new session continues from the returned work copy (inline), with the rest of the attempt's turns, seeds, GPU-seconds and wall clock, and a brief that carries the gate's word, up to five reasons and the plain gate's verdict (Δ223). Spent caps end the run by that cap. The agent's in-container tool runs are not `Verify`s. The outcome is the plain lab gate's verdict (Δ215).
  - **Exits:** completed is scored. A failed step is `infra-failed` before any gated request and `truncated:container` after one. The wall clock during a command is scored, ended by wall-clock. A deferral is `deferred` (the batch stops) before output and `truncated:host-memory` after it. A session without any request is `infra-failed`. SIGINT/SIGTERM give `truncated:interrupted` after a request, else `infra-failed`. Sessions write their gate counts into `seg-<k>/`, so `fvh resume` settles a killed runner's attempt from them, keeping the plan's `lab_task` and removing the hand-over files. An attempt that produced output is never re-issued (Δ208; no best-of-n).
  - **`fvh.backends.agent`** (new, frozen): reaches `dvl.sandbox` only through `scripts/mksandbox-run` (the core imports only the standard library). The child process gets SIGKILL when the runner dies (`PR_SET_PDEATHSIG`). `FVH_NO_MODEL` and `DVL_NO_DOCKER` refuse. The batch holds the GPU calendar lock for the whole batch and passes its locked fd to each session (`--calendar-fd`, checked by `dvl.sandbox.calendar_held_by`, never trusted).
  - **`dvl.sandbox` and `mksandbox-run`** (stable-interface additions, backward-compatible): `build_agent`, `task_files`, `AGENT_BRIEF`, `agent_model` and `calendar_held_by`; `live_run` gains the `seeds`, `gate_dir` and `calendar_held` keywords; `start_gate` gains `out`. `validate` accepts an agent run's inline editable benchmark-task file (a continuation), and the self-test requires that copy's card to be attested. New forms `--build-agent` and `--agent-session`. `fvh.report`'s lever-less disclosure now covers agent-mode lab runs; `fvh.batch.reconcile` keeps a settled run's `lab_task` in both modes.
  - **The leak:** `tests/sandbox/test_run_capped_container.py`'s `run_job` never removed its `out_dir` (13 `/tmp/dvl0-sandbox-job-*` per suite run). Fixed, with the `NoLeftovers` regression test (it fails on the old code). The host's 182 stale directories (14 runs' worth, none newer than an hour, none open) were removed; the listing is archived.
- **Why:** core tests 535 passed (12 new); `tests/sandbox` 157 OK (12 new), also under CI's sterile variables; `tests/ci/test_ci_dv4` 9 OK; `check_single_route.py` clean. Live smoke on invented tasks only (a scratch root; archive `dv5d/agentbatch/`): the precheck in containers (task forms fail, answers verify, a lever caught); a lab plan with one 12-turn agent arm × 2 tasks (a Dafny file, a Verus crate) × 2 repeats: one attempt verified; the runner SIGKILLed alone mid-session left no container, gate or run directory; `fvh resume` settled that attempt `truncated:interrupted` from its session's counts (2 calls, 6.4 GPU-s), ran the rest (failed at the call cap) and closed the batch; arm G with gate v2's reference on the crate failed at the call cap after gate v2's 4 container instruments (12.6 min). `fvh report` 5/5 reproduced, chain intact; `--reverify all` 4/4. Qwen 50 calls, 524 GPU-s; 12-turn sessions measured 70–171 GPU-s (5.9–14.3 GPU-s a turn, growing with context); the model unloaded after each leg. The live smoke did not reach arm G's refused-stop continuation (the model spent all 12 turns first); core and fake-host tests cover it, and its manifest and self-test were checked on the real tools.
- **Touches:** Δ148–Δ150, Δ182, Δ202, Δ208, Δ214–Δ217; DV4-PLAN §3.5, §4.3, §5.10, §13; QPLAN §28.5–§28.6; `CLAUDE.md`'s Not-built line; `harness/fvh/{batch,report}.py`, `harness/fvh/backends/agent.py`, `scripts/dvl/sandbox.py`, `scripts/mksandbox-run`, `harness/tests/core/test_batch.py`, `tests/sandbox/{test_agent_batch,test_agent_run,test_live_run,test_run_capped_container}.py`.
- **Owner:** DV5d's agent-mode lane.

### Δ220 · The grader's judging step as built: `truthcheck judge`, its host half and `fvh report --judge`; the archives re-sealed with the frozen oracles; the images rebuilt; the prose references judged; Q15.H4v admitted — adopted (DV5d, 2026-10-12)
- **Status:** closes Δ208's and Δ217's DV4 item (a `truthcheck` judging mode, the image rebuild, the prose references judged in the container), Δ195's (Q13.H3v's hidden check), Δ197's and Δ199's pending hidden check of Q15's prose items, Δ215's "the judging step is still not built" and QPLAN §30 Q49's last part. Like Δ195–Δ217, it names no answer, no reference statement and no wrong statement. Detail: `Solutions/plans/DV5d-judge-notes.md`.
- **Change:**
  - **`truthcheck judge <LAB>`** (`sandbox/image/truthcheck.py`): judges every candidate the host stages (`judge/candidates.json`) against its sealed reference, with the frozen reference oracles each archive now carries (`judge/oracles.py`, a byte copy of the oracles' twin; never in an image or a learner-visible file). A Lab 2 card gets the oracles' own implication, vacuity and classification (QPLAN §25.5's hidden verdict). A prose item gets a two-way refinement check (four lemmas: each side's requires gives the other's, and each side's ensures, under them, gives the other's; plus the vacuity lemma): `faithful`, `strong`, `weak`, `vacuous` or `undecided` (the cards keep the oracles' own verdict, which the learner's oracles are measured against; why the prose items take another check is in the judge notes §1). `mismatch` for a changed signature or a delivery the host refused (a changed frozen statement, a changed task-form definition or lever, an unreadable declaration); `context` when the candidate's own definitions do not verify. A reference definition the candidate states word for word is the candidate's; another is renamed. Output: verdict and outcome words per candidate and fixed reasons; nothing on stderr; every exception a fixed message. The lemmas have empty bodies, so a difference that needs an induction is `undecided` (unfaithful either way; `/review-code`'s reader decides it).
  - **Host half and commands:** `scripts/dvl/judge.py` (extraction through the course parsers, the freeze and task-form checks, the manifest); `scripts/mksandbox-run --truth ARCHIVE --truth-check judge --judge SPEC [--mem-gb N]`; `dvl.sandbox.TRUTH_MODES` gains `judge`; `python -m fvh report <ID> --judge RUN|all --truth ARCHIVE` (new frozen module `fvh.judging`: a lab's runs' deliveries, one container per language, verdicts printed, nothing written into a run directory; the archive is named on the line, so guard rule 1c refuses it in a study session; runs of an unscored or excluded outcome are not judged, Δ223). Stable-interface additions (DV4-PLAN §13): the `judge` mode, `fvh.judging` and `Judged`.
  - **Re-sealed:** `Hidden/labs/Q13/`, `Q15/` (with a `DVLCANARY`) and `Q17/`, each with `judge/oracles.py`; `sandbox/truth.toml` re-pinned. A re-seal after the oracles' twin changes must copy the file again. **Images rebuilt** (twice the same day; the evidence is from the final images): `dvl-toolchain@sha256:ee8b0f7c…03de`, `dvl-agent@sha256:bb5b5439…c79e`; `images_lock_sha256` (`9d491d55…6976`) re-pinned in `sandbox/apparatus.toml`; `VERSIONS.md` §1.4.
  - **Admissions:** **Q15.H4v admitted at 18** (QPLAN §25.7's planned minutes: 34 proof lines, inside the intermediate Verus band; client and prose proof gates; stable; pinned APIs; its hidden check faithful). ### Δ221 · The public pools validated and pinned; HumanEval-Verus's stripping step; `get_bench`'s transforms and resumable `--validate`; the attestation scan recalibrated and 78 lab card files attested — adopted (DV5d, 2026-10-12) (the scan recalibrated again and the 78 entries re-attested under D25: Δ228)
- **Status:** QPLAN §30 Q57 and Δ217's DV5 item; Δ208's attestation item; the agent-mode lane's open item on Lab 3's 36 crate files (Δ219's preflight refuses an unattested card). Like Δ195–Δ217, it names no answer. Detail: `Solutions/plans/DV5d-pools-notes.md`.
- **Change:**
  - **`get_bench.sh --validate`** (`scripts/dvl/bench.py`). The pool rule, stricter than DV4-PLAN §7.2's: the answer passes the done check (`vcheck --gate`, verified with 0 holes, and `vaudit`, no finding); it passes the lab gate's static half as a delivery for its task form (`specfreeze --lab`, 0 findings: no statement changed or missing, no lever added or dropped); its task form fails `vcheck --gate` as shipped at the verify stage; an answer equal to its task form is no task. A job killed by memory (137/143), stopped by the net (124) or without JSON is never a verdict: its row is re-run by the next invocation. Resume-safe (`validation.partial.jsonl`; another selection, set digest or tool pins needs `--restart`), stoppable (SIGINT/SIGTERM keep the finished rows), `--workers` 1..4 (default 1) within `--mem-gb` (workers × cap ≤ the share; MemAvailable ≥ share + 15 GB at the start and cap + 15 GB before each job, else it waits), a pre-registered seeded stratified `--sample N --seed S` for a set projected over 6 h. `validation.jsonl` schema 3 opens with a header row (set digest, pins digest, selection, wall time, counts by reason and stratum). `DVL_BENCH_TMP` places get_bench's temporary trees.
  - **`get_bench.sh --pin-pool`** (author-only; rule 1b, Δ225) writes **`bench/public/POOLS.toml`** (new, tracked; rule 2f by path): per set the pool's members, their list's sha256, the counts, the set digest and the tool pins; `--check` compares an installed `validation.jsonl` with it.
  - **Transforms** (`scripts/dvl/benchstrip.py`, new; a `[[set.layout]]` row's `transform`, recorded in each set's CHANGES): `verus-strip-proofs`, HumanEval-Verus's stripping step (proof fns, proof statements, ghost lets, reveals and loop invariants removed; the specification, the code and each loop's `decreases` kept, since Verus refuses a loop without one before it verifies; a proof fn a kept line names keeps its signature and an empty body); `verus-drop-main` (the top-level `fn main` outside `verus!`, which the gate's lint refuses); `dafny-axiom-assume-to-hole` (vericoding's Dafny task forms: `assume {:axiom} false;` becomes `assume false;`, a hole the gate counts). `bench/manifest.toml` re-pins humaneval-verus (`89d53507…`) and vericoding (`0eb0ae37…`).
  - **The attestation scan** (`author_scan`, `scripts/dvl/sandbox.py`): text that any learner-visible course code file shows never counts, and a hit is a whole span of a twin's answer lines of at least 10 tokens (`SPAN_TOKENS`), not one shared 7-token shingle; the lab cards' references (`Solutions/bench/local/`) are twins. `attest_data` attests given bytes and records a `source`. The scan is a tripwire: a line a learner-visible file also shows, or a single answer line under 10 tokens, is not caught alone (5 of 626 lines of 7–9 tokens); the leak patterns cover those cases.
  - **Attested** (`sandbox/attested.toml`, 78 entries): Lab 1's 22 and Lab 2's 20 cards; Lab 3's 36 sealed crate files, from a fresh decryption of the sealed archive into lane scratch (the archive's and its manifest's sha256 as `STRATUM.toml` pins, each file against the manifest; the plaintext removed at once), at the paths `fvh unseal Q18` writes.
- **Why:** the pools (whole sets, no sample needed, one 32 GB scope, no job killed): DafnyBench 303 of 785 (363 s, 4 workers), HumanEval-Verus 83 of 167 (188 s, 2 workers; 78 upstream files are TODO stubs), vericoding 2,264 of 2,683 (3,902 s, 2 workers; its `dafny/humaneval` stratum 143 of 151), MINIF2F-DAFNY 0 of 12 (not a pool). What the rule's parts caught: `vcheck --gate` passed a Dafny file whose only gap is `assume {:axiom}`, and the lever check allows a shipped lever, so an unchanged vericoding task form passed the lab gate (probed; closed for the pools by the transform; Δ224 item 4); 35 vericoding answers hold `assume {:axiom}`; 24 answers change or drop a statement and 3 add a lever; 18 DafnyBench task forms do not parse. A live SIGTERM stop kept 89 rows and left no child. The scan: the first unit refused every lab card; as built the 42 cards and the 36 sealed files pass, and each of the 2,934 twin answer lines of 10 tokens or more that no learner-visible file shows is caught when planted into a card. The leak scan of the 3,647 fetched answers (Q57): 64 files hold a whole twin answer span, 42 distinct spans, all generic idioms, none course-specific; the sets stay `fetch`. Agent manifests staging Lab 2's d01 and v01 and Lab 1's dh4 by `src` pass `--selftest-only` and `--author --selftest-only`. Tests: `tests/bench` 34 (23 new), `tests/sandbox` 5 new; `ci.sh --author --job harness` (core 541, sandbox 164, bench 34) and `--job static` PASS; the leak verifier OK (archive `dv5d/pools/`).
- **Touches:** Δ103, Δ152, Δ182, Δ208, Δ215, Δ217; DV4-PLAN §5.4 items 3 and 5, §7.1–§7.3; QPLAN §22.6, §25.6, §30 Q57, Q63; `CLAUDE.md`'s Not-built line; `scripts/dvl/{bench,benchstrip,sandbox}.py`, `scripts/get_bench.sh`, `bench/manifest.toml`, `bench/public/POOLS.toml`, `sandbox/attested.toml`, `tests/bench/`, `tests/sandbox/test_sandbox.py`.
- **Owner:** DV5d's pools lane.

### Δ223 · The DV5d review fix round: the agent route's live flag and killed sessions, the refused stop's brief, the judging tally, the ungated-admission check, the `statement-item` kind's contractor, the plan field's LaTeX forms, Lab 3's agent-mode text — adopted (DV5d, 2026-10-13)
- **Status:** three reviews of the DV5d round (harness, admission, leakage and claims) raised 18 findings (8 major, 10 minor); the fix lane reproduced 17 (the attestation scan's limit is a property of its design, recorded in Δ221), fixed all 8 majors and 8 minors in full and one minor in part (the records: Δ225 finishes them or lists them). Like Δ195–Δ217, it names no answer. Detail: `Solutions/plans/DV5d-review-fix-notes.md`.
- **Change:**
  - **`fvh.batch`'s live flag** is fixed at construction (`Batch._live`: no test transport and no test agent route injected). Under `fvh run`, `open()`'s preflight builds the live agent route, and the batch still takes the GPU calendar lock for the whole batch (its fd passed to every session), checks the model server (`model_server_found` in run.json), reaps stale per-call state and unloads the model at the end.
  - **A killed agent session's spend:** `fvh.backends.agent` reads the session's `gate-counts.json` on every return without the sandbox's record (a timeout, a failed or refused sandbox, a failed self-test); exit 137/143 or a signal is `killed-memory`. The batch decides "had output" from the counts or the run directory: after output a killed session is `truncated:host-memory` and another failure `truncated:container`, scored and never re-issued (Δ208); before output `infra-failed`.
  - **Agent-mode arm G's refused stop** gives the next session the gate's word, up to five reasons and the plain gate's verdict. The brief no longer says the in-container `vcheck --gate` checks the task "the way it is judged": the judgement is the lab gate's (with the lever check) outside the container, and a gated arm's stops are the acceptance gate's.
  - **An agent arm's wall clock:** `load_plan` refuses `wall_s` < 120 s; an attempt with no room for any session is `deferred` (the batch stops), never a scored `error`.
  - **`fvh report --judge`** neither judges nor counts a run of an unscored or excluded outcome (`runlog.UNSCORED`, `EXCLUSIONS`); it lists it as excluded (`Judged.excluded`, default false).
  - **`firsttry --empty-body` fails on an ungated admission:** an admitted H item that holds no hole and carries no `statement-item` exception prints `? <ID>  admitted with no hole and no statement-item exception: no gate` and fails the run (`ungated_items`); a sweep over Q01–Q18 finds none.
  - **The `statement-item` kind's contractor** (D6): `check_course_map.py` accepts the kind on an item of one of the module's agentic files, or on a prose item whose block names its contractor in one `// contractor: <ID>` line (an A item of the same module in an agentic file). Q17.H3d's block (learner file and twin) names `Q17.A1d`; Q15's Lab 2 prose specifications, which name no contractor, cannot take the kind (a `test_course_map.py` case).
  - **The plan field's forms:** `fvh.prereg` reads `plan-sha256:` followed by blanks, `}`, `~`, `\ ` or a second `\texttt{` before the 64 hex digits (the template's own `\texttt{plan-sha256:} <hex>` now parses), never across a line break.

The chapter's plant step and §4 say the copy keeps the crate's course path at its end.

### Δ227 · The study clone root as built (D20): the clone and `DVL_SCRATCH` under `/tmp/dvl0-study-<phase>-<lane>/` in `skill_sims.py`, `calibrate.py` and `guard_doors.py`; the set-up's hook probe; rule 1's cwd message — adopted (DV5e, 2026-10-13)

Δ231 added per-case scratch, the drop-in resets and the orphaned-root refusal after a review. Like Δ195–Δ226, it names no answer. Detail: the lane's archive `dv5e/studyroot/` and the three harness READMEs.
- **Change:**
  - **Two roots.** WORK (`--work`) must lie under an author lane's scratch, `/tmp/dvl0-dv<phase>-<lane>/…` (stricter than before: any `/tmp/dvl0-*` path was accepted; every historical default still passes), and keeps the state, raw streams, prompts, emitted copies and `calls.log` under rule 1's tripwire. The session-facing half lives under the study root: WORK with its top directory renamed `/tmp/dvl0-study-<phase>-<lane>/` (`/tmp/dvl0-dv5d-simulations/q16-q18` → `/tmp/dvl0-study-dv5d-simulations/q16-q18/{clone,dvl}`), which the tripwire's pattern does not match. `tests/acceptance/guard_doors.py` holds the one source (`RX_LANE_SCRATCH`, mirroring the hook's alternative; `study_root`, `work_paths`, `run_hook`, `probe_study_clone`, `check_study_root_free`, `remove_study_root`), which `skill_sims.py` and `calibrate.py` load by path; each tool's `paths_of` keeps a pre-D20 state's own clone path.
  - **Set-up checks.** The study root must be new or empty and lie inside no other run's study root. The clone's own hook must pass a Read of `.claude/skills/instructor/CORE.md`, a Bash call in the clone and a Read under `DVL_SCRATCH`, and refuse a Read of WORK's `calls.log`; any other verdict refuses the set-up before any model call (a repeat of DV5d's attempt 1 is caught there). The results are recorded as `study_probe` beside `study_root` and `dvl_scratch` in the state and `env.json`. `skill_sims`' lane-scratch check is unchanged and counts no study root.
  - **Scratch and cleanup.** Each tool's sessions get `DVL_SCRATCH` under the study root (before, `calibrate.py` and `guard_doors.py` used the wrappers' default `/tmp/dvl-<uid>`; per case since Δ231). Cleanup deletes the clone, the whole study root and its empty parents up to `/tmp`, and WORK's raw outputs; WORK keeps `state.json` and `calls.log` (`skill_sims` also `baseline.files.json` and `state.lock`), which the lane removes after archiving.
  - **The hook, rule 1** (`.claude/hooks/spoiler-guard.sh`): a shell inside a reference tree is still told to `cd back to $ROOT first`, unless the checkout itself lies in one; then the message says the checkout is refused and the session must run from a checkout outside those trees (a study clone under `/tmp/dvl0-study-…/`). The header's paragraph on the shell's directory says so. No pattern or exit code changed; rule 1e's message has the same shape and is unchanged (reachable only when the checkout sits in another session's state).

Real set-ups, no model called (4 GB scope): `guard_doors.py setup/dryrun/cleanup` (15 hook runs, 0 unexpected; both controls exit 0) and `calibrate.py setup --modules Q01`/`cleanup` put their clones under `/tmp/dvl0-study-dv5e-studyroot/` and removed it; `skill_sims.py setup` was refused on the host, as designed, while three other DV5e lanes' scratch existed, so its set-up and cleanup ran in-process with only the host listing pointed at an empty directory (driver archived): the clone landed in and was removed from the study root, and no session started. `check_single_route.py` clean.

### Δ228 · The attestation scan recalibrated under D25: generic structure set aside, renamed copies caught; all 78 lab card files re-attested; Lab 3's Qwen preflight passes on attestation — adopted (DV5e, 2026-10-13)
- **Status:** builds the user's decision D25 (Δ226). Measured first, Δ221's scan already passed every card, but its margins were thin (one Lab 2 card held 91% of one span) and it did not see a renamed copy (15 of 626 renamed answer regions caught).
